(9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Where an official certificate is issued under paragraph (7) above in respect of any seed, no person shall market the seed unless it has been re-labelled with a label which complies with regulation 9. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 6A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Breeder’s confirmations (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-fae859fabc0261a33b28a90976093cca][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An application for the issue of a breeder’s confirmation in respect of a seed lot— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall be made in writing by the breeder to *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in such form and manner, and shall include such information, records, illustrations, material and seeds, as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may require; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall be made not later than the thirty-first day of December of the year following the year of harvest of the crop from which the seed lot is obtained or within such other time as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may allow; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall be made only for seed of a variety when an application has been made to add the variety to *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a GB Variety List or an NI Variety List *(Extent: S)*[^key-8fcfa3cd5b22bd47023524b75ee5aaf4] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Where an application for the issue of a breeder’s confirmation is made to *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in accordance with paragraph (1) above *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall deal with the application in accordance with the provisions of Schedule 2A. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A breeder’s confirmation which has been issued by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may be withdrawn by him in respect of either the whole of a seed lot or any part of a seed lot and any seeds from which a breeder’s confirmation has been so withdrawn shall be deemed not to have had a breeder’s confirmation if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the results of an official examination of the seeds in respect of which the breeder’s confirmation has been issued are declared null and void in accordance with paragraph (2) of regulation 7, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] information has been received by him which, if known to him before the breeder’s confirmation was issued, would have resulted in the application for its issue being refused on the grounds that the seeds did not meet the requirements of the seeds regulations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 6B. Importation from a country granted equivalence[^key-12f862e02a85e5218d3fab79ca9edef8][^key-d3e92fec4be98831ea4ac242271a9eb6]

*Extent: S*

Seed imported from a country granted equivalence[^key-3b795256447bd0563f2266fb7faa9b0b] must be labelled with a label approved by the Organisation for Economic Cooperation and Development for the varietal certification on the control of seed moving in international trade.

## 6C. Certification and labelling for export

*Extent: S*

(1) The Scottish Ministers may certify the quality of any seed intended for export.

(2) Following certification in accordance with paragraph (1), any seed exported must be labelled with a label approved by the Organisation for Economic Cooperation and Development for the varietal certification on the control of seed moving in international trade.

## 7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-2a83eb059923014cdb2892db747004e9][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A sample of seeds taken for the purposes of an official examination shall be taken, subject to the payment of any fees payable under seeds regulations, by a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf and in accordance with the requirements contained in Schedule 5. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1A (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-09d5731c952ea5172622479cb32f1fc2][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A sample of seed of a Conservation Variety or Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety taken for the purposes of checking compliance with these Regulations shall be taken in accordance with the requirements contained in Schedule 5. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a sample of seeds taken for the purposes of an official examination is found not to have been taken in accordance with the requirements of seeds regulations no, or no further, official examination shall be made of that sample, and any findings or results obtained in the course of any official examination of the sample of seeds already made shall be deemed null and void. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3da399da00cebf5f4ac25b10afc1fed2][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a sample of seed of a Conservation Variety or Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety taken for the purposes of an examination is found not to have been taken in accordance with the requirements of seeds regulations, no examination or no further examination shall be made of that sample and any finding shall be null and void. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sealing of packages (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-16a761fab479faebb47ce9c53f9eabba][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall market in accordance with regulation 5(1)(a) or (2) a package (other than a package of Uncertified Pre-basic Seed, a package of Standard Seed or a small package of seeds) of Pre-basic Seed, Basic Seed or Certified Seed unless it has been, not later than at the time of sampling, sealed by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf in such a manner that when the package is opened the sealing device will be broken and cannot be re-used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a package of seeds which has been sealed in accordance with paragraph (1) above or imported in accordance with regulations 5(1)(b)(ii) or (c)(ii) shall have been opened it may only be re-sealed or further re-sealed by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall market a package of Uncertified Pre-basic Seed, a package of Standard Seed or a small package of seeds unless it has been sealed with a dealing device in such a manner that when the package is opened the sealing device will be broken and cannot be re-used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(4 (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-cccffbfaada2122fde328b4cb52709e1][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall market a package of seed of a Conservation Variety or a package of Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety unless it has been sealed by the supplier using a non-reusable sealing system or some other sealing system— *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] including the use of a label or the affixing of a seal; and *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in such a manner that the package cannot be opened without damaging the sealing system or without leaving evidence of tampering on the label or the package. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 8A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sealing of packages—breeder’s confirmation (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6ad5da1c7c220ce7c14c944395c3eee9][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall have in his possession any package of seed in respect of which a breeder’s confirmation for Pre-basic Seed or Basic Seed has been applied for unless it has been sealed in accordance with this regulation by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf in such manner that when the package is opened the sealing device will be broken and cannot be re-used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Sealing under paragraph (1) above shall take place not later than at the time of sampling or, in the case of seed that is wholly or partly re-packaged, the sealing of the original package shall take place no later than that time. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall open a package of seed which has been sealed in accordance with paragraph (1) above except— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] for the purpose of sowing all the seed; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] where— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] any action carried out in relation to the package is done immediately after opening; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] some seed remains in the package after that action has been done; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the package is re-sealed under paragraph (4) below immediately after the completion of that action; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] for the purpose of re-packaging and re-sealing all of the seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a package of seeds which has been sealed in accordance with paragraph (1) above shall (in accordance with paragraph (3) above or otherwise) have been opened neither it nor any other package containing any of those seeds may be sealed, re-sealed or further re-sealed (as the case may be) except by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If the details on any label required by regulation 9A cease to be correct as a consequence of any action of the kind described in paragraph (3) above the label shall be replaced, by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf, at the time the package is re-sealed under paragraph (4) above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Labelling of packages (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-075870375579dea484e2df446a8b82cc][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to paragraph (10) below, no person shall market any seeds in accordance with regulation 5(1)(a) or (2) except in a package which, not later than at time of sealing, is labelled, inscribed or otherwise dealt with in accordance with this regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-f1ed1714d1b2ef6bf462eaf7017d5246][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] In the case of a package of seeds in respect of which *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] has issued an official certificate under regulation 6(7), paragraph (1) above shall apply as if the words “not later than at the time of sealing” were omitted. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1B (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-bf0e6d44e0e9ee48d24050d4f210a1e6][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a variety has been genetically modified, any label or document, whether official or otherwise, affixed to or accompanying a seed lot or any part of a seed lot in accordance with the provisions of this regulation, shall clearly indicate that the variety has been genetically modified. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to the exception in paragraph (3) below, a package of Pre-basic Seed or of Basic Seed or a package, other than a small package, of Certified Seed, shall have affixed on the outside an official label, as appropriate to the seeds, which shall be of at least the minimum size and of the colour specified in Part I of Schedule 6, and shall contain particulars of the matters also specified in the said Part I, such label being either an adhesive label of a label secured to the package by a sealing device approved by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], the label being affixed (in either case) by or under the supervision of a person authorised by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in that behalf. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The official label referred to in paragraph (2) above shall not be required when all of the particulars to be included therein are included in the official inner label placed in the package in accordance with paragraph (5) of this regulation and the package is sufficiently transparent to enable such particulars to be readily legible through it. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If in accordance with regulation 5(6) a person shall market Pre-basic Seed or Basic Seed of a lower percentage of germination than that specified in Schedule 4 the fact shall be stated on the official label and a suppliers label affixed to the outside of the package shall contain a statement of the germination of the seeds together with the name and address of the seller and the reference number of the lot. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A package of Pre-basic Seed or of Basic Seed or a package, other than a small package, of Certified Seed shall contain an official inner label which shall be of the same colour as, and in addition to, the official label affixed to the outside of the package in accordance with paragraph (2) above. The official inner label shall contain particulars of the reference number of the lot, and of the kind and the variety of the seeds; except that if such particulars are printed indelibly on the outside of the package or if the official label is an adhesive or a tear-resistant label the official inner label referred to in this paragraph shall not be required. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A package of Uncertified Pre-basic Seed or a package, other than a small package, of Standard Seed shall have affixed on the outside a label, as appropriate to the seeds, which shall be of at least the minimum size and of the colour specified in Part II of Schedule 6 and which shall contain particulars of the matters also specified in the said Part II; except that if all such particulars are printed indelibly on the outside of the package the said label shall not be required and if some of such particulars are so printed they may be omitted from the said label. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A small package of Certified Seed or a small package of Standard Seed shall have affixed on the outside a label, as appropriate to the seeds, which shall be of the colour specified in Part III of Schedule 6 and which shall contain particulars of the matters also specified in the said Part III; except that if all such particulars are printed indelibly on the outside of the package the said label shall not be required and if some of such particulars are so printed they may be omitted from the said label. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a package of Pre-basic Seed, Basic Seed or Certified Seed shall have been re-sealed in accordance with regulation 8(2) above this fact shall be stated on the official label together with the month and year of re-sealing and the name of the authority responsible for the re-sealing either on the official label referred to in paragraph (2) above or on the official inner label in the circumstances referred to in paragraph (3) above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to the exception in paragraphs (12B) and (12C) in respect of seed of a Conservation Variety and Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety, *(Extent: S)*[^key-d0d27bf1c350635417291d9e4fb87377] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] if any seeds have been subjected to any chemical treatment this fact and the nature of the treatment or the proprietary name of the chemical used in the treatment shall be stated either— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] on a separate supplier’s label, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] on the label referred to in paragraph (2), (6) or (7) above or, where such a label is not required, with the particulars otherwise given in accordance with those paragraphs, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

and also, except where the label referred to in paragraph (2), (6) or (7) above is not required or where the information prescribed by this paragraph is given on an adhesive or tear-resistant label, either on the outside of the package or in a statement enclosed in the package. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-42aa11dcc07b779690ee667e5dd156af][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Except in relation to seed of a Conservation Variety or Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety, *(Extent: S)*[^key-c7709b049767ad0102d8c83bde0b5b78] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a person may sell any seeds otherwise than in a package which is labelled, inscribed or otherwise dealt with in accordance with this regulation where— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the seeds are sold by way of retail sale in quantity not exceeding the appropriate maximum weight indicated in Schedule 7, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the seeds are taken, in the presence of the purchaser, from a container on which there is clearly and visibly marked or near which there is clearly and visibly displayed a statement containing particulars of the matters specified in *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Part V *(Extent: E)*[^key-dca61f51c844f2e1e3293b8e8c712da1] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] of Schedule 6. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(12 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The particulars and information given in accordance with this regulation shall be given in *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] English but may also be given in other languages *(Extent: S)*[^key-ec20b355daceb0e8e4a3b2c4efa753df] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(12A (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-526c283cc7fdb2edf5a1649a2fc1c2a7][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Where the kind or variety of seed is indicated on a label or marking it must be in Roman characters and the kind of seed may be designated by common name only or by common name and Latin name. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(12B (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5cce3d56343169df4fd9dd9f120c5455][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A package of seed of a Conservation Variety shall bear a supplier’s label or a printed or stamped notice containing the information specified in Part VI of Schedule 6 (and for the avoidance of doubt neither an official label nor an official inner label is required). *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(12C (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A package of Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety shall bear a supplier’s label or a printed or stamped notice containing the information specified in Part VII of Schedule 6 (and for the avoidance of doubt neither an official label nor an official inner label is required). *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(13 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to the provisions of the Act and of these Regulations, no person shall, in the course of the marketing or the preparation for marketing of any seeds by himself or by any other person, wilfully reproduce, remove, alter, deface, conceal or misuse in any way any label affixed to, contained in or marked on any package of seeds or to be so affixed, contained or marked. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 9A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Labelling of packages—breeder’s confirmation (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-fad7fa966ec0dfbb30d7665034235391][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] No person shall have in his possession any package of seed in respect of which a breeder’s confirmation for Pre-basic Seed or Basic Seed has been applied for unless it has been, not later than the time of sealing under regulation 8A, labelled, inscribed or otherwise dealt with in accordance with paragraphs (2) to (9) of this regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to the exception in paragraph (3) below, packages of seeds shall have affixed on the outside an official label containing, as appropriate to the seeds, particulars of the matters specified, and being at least the size and of the colour also specified in Part I of Schedule 6 and over-stamped “not certified”, which shall be either an adhesive label or a label secured to the package by a sealing device approved by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The official label referred to in paragraph (2) above shall not be required when all of the particulars to be included therein are included in the official inner label placed in the package in accordance with paragraph (5) below and the package is sufficiently transparent to enable such particulars to be readily legible through it. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If in accordance with paragraph 2 of Schedule 2A (applications for breeder’s confirmation) a breeder’s confirmation shall have been issued in respect of seed of a lower percentage of germination than that specified in Schedule 4 the fact shall be stated on the official label and where possession of the package is transferred to another person, the transferor shall affix a label to the outside of the package containing a statement of the germination of the seeds together with his name and address and the reference number of the lot. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A package shall contain an official inner label which shall be of the same colour as, and in addition to the official label affixed to the outside of the package in accordance with paragraph (2) above. The official inner label shall contain particulars of the reference number of the lot and of the kind of seed and the variety; except that if such particulars are printed indelibly on the outside of the package or if the official label is an adhesive or tear-resistant label, an official inner label shall not be required. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If a package of seeds shall have been re-sealed in accordance with regulation 8A(4) this fact shall be stated on the official label, together with the month and year of re-sealing and the name of the authority responsible for the re-sealing. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If any seeds have been subjected to any chemical treatment this fact and the nature of the treatment or the proprietary name of the chemical used in the treatment shall be stated either— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] on a separate label, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] on the label referred to in paragraph (2) or (4) above, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] where paragraph (3) above applies, on the official inner label referred to in that paragraph, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

and also, except where the information prescribed by this paragraph is given on an adhesive or tear-resistant label, either on the outside of the package or in a statement enclosed in the package. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The particulars and information given in accordance with this regulation shall be given in *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] English but may also be given in other languages *(Extent: S)*[^key-8bf8ba1dc6b7dcac15f958f03f75eef7] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(8A (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-17150b64c113613f085d0275e0faca1b][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Where the kind or variety of seed is indicated on a label or marking it must be in Roman characters and the kind of seed may be designated by common name only or by common name and Latin name. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] For the purposes of this regulation, references in Schedule 6 to the “Certifying Authority” shall, in the case of a breeder’s confirmation, be taken to be references to that authority as issuer of the confirmation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Subject to the provisions of these Regulations, no person shall wilfully reproduce, remove, alter, deface, conceal or misuse in any way any label affixed to, contained in or marked on any package of seeds or which is to be so affixed, contained or marked, except as may occur in the course of opening the package for the purpose of sowing or for any other lawful purpose. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Record of transactions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-b09f1c0c14440329960fd1e618900284][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A person who in the course of marketing any Standard Seed affixes or causes to be affixed, in accordance with regulation 9(6), a label on the outside of a package of Standard Seed or prints or stamps or causes to be printed or stamped on the outside of such a package the particulars referred to in the said regulation 9(6) shall maintain and keep available for inspection by an officer of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a record of the dates on which and of the packages on which such labels were affixed or particulars printed or stamped and shall, if so required by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], furnish him with a copy of such record. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A person referred to in paragraph (1) above shall keep and retain for a period of at least three years a record of the seed lots of Standard Seed marketed by him, and shall take and retain for a period of at least two years a sample of each such seed lot of at least the appropriate minimum weight specified in Part II of Schedule 5 and, if so required by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], or by an officer of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], shall produce such record or deliver any such sample to him. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Civil liabilities of sellers of seeds (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-96195d8cc0d6a736c35677bdd6aa1034][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The particulars given to a purchaser by the seller of seeds to which these Regulations apply, whether given in pursuance of these Regulations expressly or by implication arising from the description under which the seeds are sold, shall constitute a statutory warranty for the purpose of section 17 of the Act in so far as they relate to the category of the seeds, the percentage germination of the seeds, the percentage analytical purity of the seeds, the content of seeds of other plant species and the varietal identity and the varietal purity of the seeds or, in the case of a mixture of seeds permitted by regulation 5(2), of each of its constituents to which these Regulations apply. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Section 17(2) of the Act shall apply to any particulars given to a purchaser by the seller of seeds, being particulars given or implied as in paragraph (1) above, in so far as they relate to the percentage germination, the percentage analytical purity, or the content of seeds of other plant species and there are hereby prescribed in respect of such matters the limits of variation set out in Schedule 8. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Section 17(3) of the Act shall apply to any particulars given to a purchaser by the seller of seeds, being particulars given or implied as in paragraph (1) above, in so far as they relate to the percentage germination, the percentage analytical purity or the content of seeds of other plant species. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A purchaser who intends to obtain a test of seeds for the purpose of section 17(3) of the Act shall, not more than 10 days after delivery to him of the seeds, give to the seller written notice of his intention and thereupon the seller may indicate a day (not being more than 21 days after delivery of the seeds to the purchaser) and a reasonable time on that day at which a sample of the seeds may be taken in the presence of himself or of his representative and the purchaser shall afford to the seller reasonable facilities for that purpose. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] On the day and at the time appointed by the seller in accordance with paragraph (4) above or, if the seller shall have failed to appoint such a day and time, on a day not more than 28 days after delivery of the seeds to the purchaser, the purchaser or his representative may, and if the seller or his representative is present shall, take a sample of the seeds. Such sample shall be taken and divided by the purchaser or his representative into two parts in accordance with the requirements contained in Part I of Schedule 5, each part being of at least the appropriate minimum weight specified in Part II of Schedule 5, of which one part shall be sent to the Chief Officer of an official seed testing station for the purpose of being tested and the other part delivered or tendered to the seller or his representative or, if he or his representative was not present when the sample was taken, sent to him by post. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## 11A. Certification in a Crown Dependency or a country granted equivalence[^key-fd89ffebca146cac06cc3da7fd496a66]

*Extent: S*

Any seed certified and labelled in a Crown Dependency or a country granted equivalence under legislation recognised by the Scottish Ministers to have equivalent effect to these Regulations may be marketed in Scotland.

## 11B. Transitional provision for official labels and supplier's labels on IP completion day

*Extent: S*

A label pre-printed before IP completion day which at the date on which it was printed was an official label or a supplier's label for the purposes of these Regulations, is to be treated as an official label or, as the case may be, a supplier's label for packages of Basic Seed, Certified Seed, Standard Seed, Seed of a Conservation Variety or Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety or, as the case may be, small packages of Certified Seed or Standard Seed, for the purposes of any use of that label before the end of the period of twelve months beginning with the day after the day on which IP completion day falls.

In witness whereof the Official Seal of the Minister of Agriculture, Fishers and Food is hereunto affixed on 30th July 1993. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

## SCHEDULE 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — OFFICIAL CERTIFICATES (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Regulations 3(1) and (3), 5(4), (6) and (12) and 6(2) *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-d28ef57933d08f06856b7e2710c285b3][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] On receipt of an application in accordance with regulation 6(1) for the issue of an official certificate in respect of a seed lot of Pre-basic Seed, Basic Seed *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] , Certified Seed or Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety *(Extent: S)*[^key-2b2820ca358336da5a6c48e69a65df8f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall, subject to paragraphs 2 and 3 below and to the payment of any fees payable under seeds regulations, issue in respect of such a seed lot an official certificate containing the particulars specified in Schedule 3. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-11d4fa1805e0bc2eaf4bc2ed863130f9][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Except in the case of Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety, *(Extent: S)*[^key-cd8f40859e8aa16dee65295c4e1bf1af] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall refuse to issue an official certificate in respect of a seed lot unless— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an application has been made to *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], in such form and manner and at such time as he may require, for registration by him of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the seed lot or seed lots to be used for the production of the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of the crop or crops from which the seed lot was obtained shall have shown that the crop or crops meet the standards appropriate to the category set out in Part I of Schedule 4; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of a sample of the seed lot shall have shown that the seeds meet the standard appropriate to the category set out in Part II of Schedule 4; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

  except that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] sub-paragraph (c) above, in so far as it relates to standards of germination set out in Part II of Schedule 4, shall not apply in relation to an application for the issue of an official certificate in respect of seeds which are marketed in accordance with and subject to the requirements of regulation 5(6), and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] shall issue an official certificate in respect of a seed lot marketed in accordance with, and subject to any conditions imposed by, a general licence granted by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] under regulation 5(4). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

*Extent: S*

**2A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-15fe93f39e25f5f619641b7ea58be9bc][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] In the case of Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety, the Ministers shall refuse to issue an official certificate in respect of a seed lot unless— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an application has been made to the Ministers, in such form and manner and at such time as they may require, for registration by them of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the seed lot or seed lots to be used for the production of the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an examination of the crop of crops from which the seed lot was obtained shall have shown that the crop or crops meets paragraphs 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 and 7 of Part I of Schedule 4; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an examination of a sample of the seed lot shows that the seed meets paragraphs 2 and 3 of Part II of Schedule 4. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-bcc274b6c9c45e35368da89162f93830][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Except in the case of Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety, *(Extent: S)*[^key-a8e238d3cef1f81f3fd4674dd8c5fd8b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may refuse to issue an official certificate in respect of a seed lot if it appears to him that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a sample of the seed lot taken for the purpose of an official examination in order to ascertain whether the seed lot meets the appropriate standards set out in Part II of Schedule 4 has not been taken in accordance with the requirements contained in Schedule 5; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of a plot sown with a sample of the seed lot sown in the field shows that the crop does not meet the appropriate standards set out in Part I of Schedule 4; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] there has been any breach of seeds regulations in relation to the seed lot in respect of which application for an official certificate has been made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

*Extent: S*

**3A.**[^key-a741a56584a6346b12bb959ff71f138c] In the case of Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety the Ministers may refuse to issue an official certificate in respect of a seed lot if it appears to them that—

- (a) a sample of the seed lot taken for the purpose of an examination in order to ascertain whether the seed lot meets the appropriate standards set out in paragraphs 2 and 3 of Part II of Schedule 4 has not been taken in accordance with the requirements contained in Schedule 5;
- (b) official post control shows that the seed does not have sufficient varietal purity; or
- (c) there has been any breach of seeds regulations in relation to the seed lot in respect of which an application for an official certificate has been made.

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an application has been made to *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c], at such time as he may require, for registration by him of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the seed lot or seed lots to be used for the production of the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the crop or crops from which the seed lot is to be obtained; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of the crop or crops from which the seed lot was obtained shall have shown that the crop or crops meet the standards appropriate to the category and the level set out in Part I of Schedule 4; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of a sample of the seed lot shall have shown that the seeds meet the standards appropriate to the category and the level set out in Part II of Schedule 4; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

except that paragraph 2(c) above, in so far as it relates to standards of germination set out in Part II of Schedule 4, shall not apply in relation to the issue of a breeder’s confirmation for Pre-basic Seed or Basic Seed in respect of seeds which attain a lower percentage of germination than that specified in Schedule 4. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An application under paragraph 2(a) above shall be in writing in such form and manner as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may require and shall be accompanied by such information and records as he may require in order to satisfy himself as to the identity of the seed lot or lots or crop or crops to be registered. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may refuse to issue a breeder’s confirmation in respect of a seed lot if it appears to him— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a sample of the seed lot taken for the purpose of an official examination in order to ascertain whether the seed lot meets the appropriate standards set out in Part II of Schedule 4 has not been taken in accordance with the requirements contained in Schedule 5; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] an official examination of a plot sown with a sample of the seed lot sown in the field shows that the crop does not meet the appropriate standards set out in Part I of Schedule 4; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] there has been a breach of seeds regulations in relation to the seed lot in respect of which application for a breeder’s confirmation has been made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-e3ae243178c478d34d6b37f33e72e5b4][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] So far as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] can ascertain them, by the use of methods which shall include official examination of the crop and which may, at such stages in the production of a variety as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may require, include examination of a plot sown with a sample from the seed lot sown in the field and the consideration of any other relevant information, the requirements for the crop set out below shall be met. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Varietal identity and varietal purity (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-b73801c11aceedc72cc9438e335bea91][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The characteristics for the determination of varietal identity and varietal purity shall be— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in respect of an official certificate, those recognised as those of the variety for the purposes of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a GB Variety List or an NI Variety List *(Extent: S)*[^key-7a1934bc276b952d1fe8b72b8caf5166] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] in respect of a breeder’s confirmation, those recognised as those of the variety used in testing for the purposes of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] a GB Variety List or an NI Variety List. *(Extent: S)*[^key-ba7702e00201f931813bd0aa98598c49] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Crop inspections (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d8751744254df4e1038b05e921c03e47][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The crop shall be officially examined in such manner and at such times (when the cultural condition of the field and the stage of development and condition of the crop are such as to permit suitable checks of varietal identity, varietal purity and species purity to be made) as *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] may reasonably require. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The crop must— *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] be practically free from any pests which reduce the usefulness and quality of the propagation material; and *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] comply with the requirements concerning *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] GB *(Extent: S)*[^key-01bcd8cefef3688a1a04f6c530b6c916] *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] quarantine pests, *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] PFA *(Extent: S)*[^key-f4e2449630762ee574d744c221d037b2] *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] quarantine pests and RNQPs provided for in implementing acts adopted pursuant to the EU Plant Health Regulation, as well as with the measures adopted pursuant to Article 30(1) of that Regulation. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Previous cropping (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-cc205870ef06477b4a68c4d03ef28780][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The crop may be grown only on land which has not previously been cropped in a manner that might adversely affect the nature or quality of the seeds to be produced and which complies with *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers' *(Extent: S)*[^M_F_2f88a651-0403-4507-ad66-fa2dbb47b6ae] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] requirements in that respect. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Isolation distances (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-87ec3e2a6a60c12b53456700889beace][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

There shall be either a physical barrier or at least 2 metres of fallow between the seed crop and any crop likely to cause contamination in the seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (c (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-64bf281848fde2038363017a39af6085][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] In the case of varieties of *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] *Zea mays *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]* (Sweet corn – super-sweet types) the required minimum germination capacity is reduced to 80% of pure seed. The official label or the supplier’s label, where appropriate, shall be endorsed ‘minimum germination capacity 80%’. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**3 (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-38a4d65410b5f5444f78ce152042da3f][^key-58eed67a8c3957383d34b13d35e483b5][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Seed must— *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] be practically free from any pests which reduce the usefulness and quality of the propagation material; and *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (b (Extent: S) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] comply with the requirements concerning *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] GB *(Extent: S)*[^key-d482df7498a1293c3feede3adfb39bb9] *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] quarantine pests, *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] PFA *(Extent: S)*[^key-39bba6a667ae40c9ac2787bb21e80f9a] *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] quarantine pests and RNQPs provided for in implementing acts adopted pursuant to the EU Plant Health Regulation, as well as with the measures adopted pursuant to Article 30(1) of that Regulation. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

### PART III — EXAMINATIONS USED TO ASCERTAIN WHETHER A CROP OR SEED LOT MEETS THE CONDITIONS RELATING TO CERTIFIED SEED OF A CONSERVATION VARIETY, STANDARD SEED OF A CONSERVATION VARIETY AND STANDARD SEED OF AN AMATEUR VARIETY[^key-2bcb58bb4dfba2548f08efef3c98d747]

*Extent: S*

All examinations of Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety, Standard Seed of a Conservation Variety and Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety used to ascertain whether the crops or seed lots meet the standards in this Schedule applicable to the category shall be carried out in accordance with current international methods insofar as such methods  exist.

## SCHEDULE 5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Regulations 3(1), 7(1) and (2) and 11(5) *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

### PART I (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

#### SAMPLING OF SEED LOTS (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-657bdce0d1c9e87ed5d8b89dd839f13d][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] A sample shall be obtained from the seed lot by taking primary samples at random from different positions in the lot and combining them to form a composite sample. The composite sample may be submitted for testing intact but if the composite sample is too large it may be reduced in weight by using an approved seed sample divider, to give the submitted sample. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Primary sample size (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-05171771d7710e7f01c860bfb7d374b9][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

At each position of sampling of a seed lot, primary samples of approximately equal size shall be taken. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Condition of the Seed Lot (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-2ed9c37807b7070982235b883e73fe49][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The seed lot to be sampled shall have been subject to appropriate mixing and blending techniques so that it is as uniform as practicable. There shall be no documentary or other evidence of heterogeneity. If a seed lot is presented for sampling in more than one container, the containers shall be of the same size and type and contain approximately the same weight of seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling from Sacks (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-169b49e5c2c592c12a3b304c08605a7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

When the seed lot is in sacks or similar sized containers each containing more than 15 kg of seed and not more than 100 kg of seed, the minimum number of containers to be sampled shall be in accordance with the following table:— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

| Number of containers in the lot *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Minimum number of containers to be sampled *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| --- | --- |
| 1-5 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Sample each container and always take at least five primary samples *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 6-30 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Sample five containers or at least one in every three containers, whichever is the greater *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 31-400 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Sample 10 containers or at least one in every five containers, whichever is the greater *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 401 or more *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Sample 80 containers or at least one in every seven containers, whichever is the greater *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |

**5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-671929a2f3cbd4f0fafecebf9f6d7350][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The containers to be sampled shall be selected at random and primary samples drawn from the top, middle and the bottom of containers, but not necessarily from more than one position in any container. The position from which the seed is taken shall be varied from container to container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling from small containers (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-2a8ea69c4fbed825398199243a7660c0][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

For sampling seed lots in containers holding 15 kg of seed or less, a 100 kg weight of seed shall be taken as the basic unit and the small containers shall be combined to form sampling units not exceeding this weight (eg six packages of 15 kg, 20 packages of 5 kg). For sampling purposes each unit shall be regarded as one container and the sampling procedures prescribed in paragraphs 4 and 5 above shall be used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-84002c7bb86b11b0042c1304a5a50815][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] When seed is in moisture-proof containers the opened or pierced containers shall be adequately closed or the residues from sampling transferred to new containers. When seeds are in very small packets (100 g or less) each packet may be considered as a primary sample and sufficient shall be taken at random to obtain a submitted sample of the minimum size prescribed in Part II of this Schedule. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling from large containers or seed in bulk (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-e866ca70e71235be1de26b897dd210df][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

For the sampling of containers holding more than 100 kg of seed, and for the sampling of seed in bulk, primary samples shall be taken from different horizontal and vertical positions selected at random and the following shall be regarded as the minimum requirement:— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

| Lot weight *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | Number of primary samples to be taken *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| --- | --- |
| up to 500 kg *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | at least five primary samples *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 501-3000 kg *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | one primary sample for each 300 kg but not less than five *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 3001-20,000 kg *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | one primary sample for each 500 kg but not less than 10 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |
| 20,001 kg and above[^key-08d59ff036845b35843b4f44314ce61d] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] | one primary sample for each 700 kg but not less than 40 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] |

##### 9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling from a seed stream (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-facef178f086e56f4a6ec51ec3ac55d0][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Samples may be drawn from a seed stream during processing using an automatic sampling device, which shall uniformly sample the entire cross-section of the seed stream when a sample is taken. Portions of seed shall be taken at regular intervals throughout the processing of the lot using the same sampling intensity as for seed in bulk (see paragraph 8 above). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Sampling Instruments (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-643909f26c8470984a6bc1ae21301bd8][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Sampling instruments shall be capable of sampling all parts of the seed lot. Where it is not practicable to use one of the instruments or methods described in this Schedule other instruments or methods may be used at the discretion of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

**11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^key-34cfd6af7c8697deb679edf96f4a176d][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument used for drawing primary samples shall be one of those described below as appropriate to the location of the seeds, or any other suitable instrument for the time being approved by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Dynamic Spear Sampler *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] This instrument may be used, in accordance with the methods described in paragraph 12(a) below, for sampling seeds in sacks or small containers. It shall be a hollow, cylindrical, solid-pointed metal spear or trier which shall be long enough to reach beyond the middle of the sack from the side and shall have an aperture so positioned that portions of seed of equal volume are removed from each part of the sack through which it travels. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Stick Samplers *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] These instruments shall have an aperture or apertures which shall be of sufficient size to allow the unrestricted entry of seeds or other particles and which shall be capable of being opened and closed during the sampling procedure as appropriate to the method of use described in paragraph 12(b). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] **Single chamber type *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**

    This instrument may be used for sampling seeds in open sacks, in large containers or in bulk. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    [^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] **Multi-chamber type *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**

    This instrument may be used to sample seeds in sacks or, subject to the requirements of paragraph 10 above, any other containers. The apertures shall open into chambers that shall be separated from one another by transverse partitions. The contents of each chamber shall be regarded as a primary sample. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    [image: see source](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/1993/2008/images/uksi_19932008_en_003)
