    [^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Cargo Sampler *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] This instrument shall only be used, in accordance with the method described in paragraph 12(c) below, for sampling seed in large containers or bulk and shall only be used in the vertical position. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Seed Stream Samplers *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Seed stream samplers shall uniformly sample the cross section of the seed stream without any loss of the seeds that enter the sampler, in accordance with the method described in paragraph 12(d) below. Timing devices shall be attached so that the frequency and duration of sampling can be adjusted to meet the requirements of paragraph 9. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 12 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Methods of Use (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-e54c2fe457d01752c1014e34c187d4e1][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

All instruments shall be clean before use. The methods of using these instruments shall be as follows:— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Dynamic Spear Sampler *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] These samplers may be used in two ways as appropriate to their length. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - **Method 1 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**(For instruments in which the aperture reaches only to the centre of the sack or small container.) *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - The instrument shall be inserted into the sack in an upward direction at an angle of approximately 30° to the horizontal with its aperture downwards until the aperture reaches the centre of the sack or container. The instrument shall be lightly tapped to remove any seed taken in and then rotated to bring the aperture uppermost. It shall be withdrawn immediately with a vibratory or oscillatory motion and at a decreasing speed so that the quantity of seed obtained from successive locations increases progressively from the centre to the side of the sack. Seed passing through the instrument shall be collected in a clean container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - **Method 2 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**(For instruments in which the aperture reaches to the far side of a sack or small container.) *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - A longer instrument shall be inserted in the manner described in Method 1 above until its aperture reaches almost to the far side of the sack or container. It shall be tapped and rotated in the manner described above and then withdrawn at a uniform speed. Seed passing through the instrument shall be collected in a clean container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Stick Sampler *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] **Single chamber type *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**
    - a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The sleeve shall move freely. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be inserted vertically downwards till the aperture reaches the appropriate primary sampling position ensuring that the sleeve covers the aperture as it enters the seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be withdrawn sufficiently to uncover the aperture. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be left in position until the primary sample has been collected. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be withdrawn and the contents emptied into a clean container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] **Multi-chamber type *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]**
    - a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The apertures shall be closed before insertion. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be inserted diagonally into sacks or vertically into large containers so that all apertures are fully covered. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The apertures shall be opened. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be agitated so that seed enters. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The apertures shall be closed gently to ensure that trapped seed is not broken or damaged. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be withdrawn and if the chambers are full the contents shall be emptied onto a clean surface or into a clean container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
    - g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If all the chambers are not full, the contents shall be discarded and procedures a-f shall be repeated. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Cargo Sampler *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The lid shall open and close easily. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be inserted with the lid closed to the appropriate primary sampling position which shall be at least 300mm below the surface of the seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The handle shall be raised sufficiently to open the lid. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be left in position until the primary sample has been collected. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The instrument shall be withdrawn and the contents shall be emptied into a clean container. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Seed Stream Samplers *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Such instruments shall be required to take the number of primary samples necessary for the weight of lot being sampled, the primary samples being taken from the lot at regular intervals and the resultant composite sample being of a weight not less than that prescribed in Part II below and not greater than can conveniently be reduced by means of one of the methods of sample reduction described in paragraph 14 below. They shall be installed in such a way that the composite sample shall be readily identifiable with the seed lot from which it was taken. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 13 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Obtaining submitted sample—composite sample division instruments (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-04da0defac2a545c35036d7cb70020eb][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Provided the composite sample is of at least the minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample for a particular kind of seed, it may be used as the submitted sample. Where the composite sample exceeds the weight prescribed for the submitted sample, any of the following instruments may be used to obtain the submitted sample. Other instruments and methods may be used at the discretion of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] the Ministers *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Riffle Divider *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The riffle divider shall consist of a rectangular hopper leading to a series of evenly spaced chutes, arranged so that seed is distributed equally on two sides. There shall be a minimum of 18 chutes, each discharging in the opposite direction to its immediate neighbour. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

  Three (or more) metal collecting vessels (pans) of sufficient depth to prevent seed bouncing out shall be used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Centrifugal Divider *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The centrifugal divider shall consist of a hopper from which seed flows on to a shallow cup which is then rotated by an electric motor. The seed shall be distributed by centrifugal force onto a stationary baffle which divides it into two equal parts which are then discharged through separate spouts. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

  Four metal collecting vessels (pans) of sufficient depth to prevent seed bouncing out shall be used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

##### 14 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Methods of Use (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-41f5e0d490c96dfaa25fe1a8266afccf][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The methods of using these instruments shall be as follows: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Riffle Divider *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The divider shall be placed on a firm level surface. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The divider and pans shall be clean. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Sample Mixing *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An empty pan shall be placed on each side of the divider to receive the discharge from the chutes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The entire composite sample shall be poured evenly into the other pan(s). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The seed shall then be poured from the pan(s) evenly along the entire length of the hopper. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The two pans into which the seed has passed shall then be replaced with empty pans. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Procedures e. and f. shall then be repeated twice to mix the sample thoroughly. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Sample Reduction *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Before reduction, the composite sample shall have been thoroughly mixed using procedures c.-g. The contents of one of the two receiving pans shall then be set aside. If the seed in the second pan is of at least the minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample of a particular kind of seed, it may be used as the submitted sample. If the weight of seed in the second pan is greater than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, it may be reduced using the following procedures i.-k. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An empty pan shall be placed on each side of the divider to receive the discharge from the chutes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- j (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of the second pan shall be poured evenly along the entire length of the hopper. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- k (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of one of the two pans into which the seed has passed shall then be set aside. If the weight of seed in the other pan is still greater than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for the submitted sample, it may be submitted intact or it may be reduced further using procedures i.-k. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If the weight of seed in either of the two pans at either h. or k. is less than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, the following procedures m.-q. shall be used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- m (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An empty pan shall be placed on each side of the divider to receive the discharge from the chutes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- n (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of one of the pans shall be set aside. The contents of the other pan shall be poured evenly along the entire length of the hopper. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- o (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] One receiving pan and its contents shall then be removed and replaced with an empty pan. The other pan, with its contents, shall be left in place. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- p (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of the pan removed at o. shall be poured evenly along the entire length of the hopper so that two subsamples of different weight shall be produced. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- q (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Procedures o. and p. above, when repeated, constitute the process of continuous halving, and shall be repeated using whichever subsample is appropriate until sufficient seed is obtained in one pan, which when added to the seed set aside at n. produces a submitted sample of at least the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Obtaining more than one submitted sample *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- r (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Two submitted samples shall be obtained from a composite sample by first obtaining a sample of at least twice the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, using procedures c.–g., and h.–q. as appropriate, and then dividing it into two parts by passing it once through the divider. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- s (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] When three submitted samples are required from one composite sample, one sample of at least the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, shall be extracted using procedures c.–g., and h.–q. as appropriate. All portions of seed which have been set aside shall then be recombined and the residue of the composite sample so obtained shall be subject to the procedure at r. above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Centrifugal Divider *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The divider shall be levelled prior to use. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
  - b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The divider and pans shall be clean. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Sample Mixing *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] An empty pan shall be placed under each spout of the divider. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The entire composite sample shall be poured into the centre of the hopper. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The motor shall be operated so that the seed passes into the pans. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The motor shall be switched off. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The two pans containing seed shall be removed and replaced by empty ones. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of both pans removed at g. shall be poured together into the centre of the hopper, the seed being allowed to blend as it flows in. Procedures e., f. and g. shall be repeated. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Procedure h. shall be repeated to mix the sample thoroughly. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Sample Reduction *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- j (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Before reduction, the composite sample shall have been thoroughly mixed using procedures c.–i. The contents of one of the two receiving pans shall then be set aside. If the seed in the second pan is of at least the minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample of a particular kind of seed, it may be used as the submitted sample. If the weight of the seed in the second pan is greater than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, it may be reduced using the following procedures (k.–m.). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- k (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Empty pans shall be placed under the spouts. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- l (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of the second pan shall be poured into the hopper and procedures e. and f. shall be repeated. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- m (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of one of the two pans into which the seed has passed shall then be set aside. If the weight of seed in the other pan is still greater than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, it may be submitted intact or it may be reduced further using procedures k.–m. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- n (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] If the weight of seed in either of the two pans at either j. or m. is less than the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, the following procedures (o.–s.) shall be used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- o (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Empty pans shall be placed under the spouts. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- p (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of one of the pans shall be set aside. The contents of the other pan shall be poured into the centre of the hopper and procedures e. and f. shall be repeated. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- q (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] One receiving pan and its contents shall then be removed and replaced with an empty pan. The other pan, with its contents, shall be left in place. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- r (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The contents of the pan removed at q. shall be poured into the centre of the hopper and procedures e. and f. shall be repeated so that two subsamples of different weight shall be produced. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- s (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Procedures q. and r. above, when repeated, constitute the process of continuous halving, and shall be repeated using whichever subsample is appropriate until sufficient seed is obtained in one pan, which when added to the seed set aside at p., produces a submitted sample of at least the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

Obtaining more than one submitted sample *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- t (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Two submitted samples shall be obtained from a composite sample by first obtaining a sample of at least twice the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, using procedures c.–i., and j.–s. as appropriate, and then dividing it into two parts by passing it once through the divider. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- u (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] When three submitted samples are required from one composite sample, one sample, of at least the appropriate minimum weight prescribed in Part II below for a submitted sample, shall be extracted using procedures c.–i., and j.–s. as appropriate. All portions of seed which have been set aside shall then be recombined and the residue of the composite sample so obtained shall be subject to procedure t. above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

### PART II (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3522416506e7da0c41bd702706230d53][^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

#### MAXIMUM WEIGHT OF A SEED LOT AND MINIMUM WEIGHT OF A SUBMITTED SAMPLE (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

The maximum weight of a seed lot shall be as indicated below (or a weight which does not exceed that indicated below by more than 5%) and the minimum weight of a submitted sample shall be as indicated below:— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    - (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] for Certified Seed— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] Minimum size of the label—110 mm × 67 mm *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c] The label shall be white for Basic Seed and Blue for Certified Seed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

### PART VI — SUPPLIER’S LABEL FOR A PACKAGE OF SEED OF A CONSERVATION VARIETY[^key-16394ea110798541f4bc9b62f7cc271e]

*Extent: S*

The supplier’s label for a package of seed of a Conservation Variety shall be a minimum size of 110 millimetres by 67 millimetres, be coloured brown and contain the following information:—

- (a) the words “GB[^M_F_60125159-60bc-4332-c590-a984cb6a2a81] rules and standards”;
- (b) the name and address of the person responsible for affixing the label or notice or that person’s identification mark;
- (c) the year of sealing, or the year of last sampling for the purposes of the last testing of germination, expressed by the word “sealed” or “sampled” (as the case may be), followed by the year in question;
- (d) the species;
- (e) the denomination of the conservation variety;
- (f) the words “Certified Seed of a Conservation Variety” or “Standard Seed of a Conservation Variety”;
- (g) the region of origin;
- (h) where the region of seed production is different from the region of origin, the indication of the region of seed production;
- (i) the reference number of the lot given by the person responsible for affixing the labels;
- (j) the declared net or gross weight or declared number of seeds;
- (k) where weight is indicated and granulated pesticides, pelleting substances or other solid additives are used, the nature of the chemical treatment or additive and the approximate ratio between the weight of clusters of pure seeds and the total weight.

### PART VII — SUPPLIER’S LABEL FOR A PACKAGE OF STANDARD SEED OF AN AMATEUR VARIETY[^key-6c38f4ef6fdea5fece203227de475789]

*Extent: S*

The supplier’s label for a package of Standard Seed of an Amateur Variety shall contain the following information:—

- (a) the words “GB[^M_F_e4246949-bfbd-46a4-8009-69311b1553af] rules and standards”;
- (b) the name and address of the person responsible for affixing the label or notice or that person’s identification mark;
- (c) the year of sealing, or the year of last sampling for the purposes of the last testing of germination, expressed by the word “sealed” or “sampled” (as the case may be), followed by the year in question;
- (d) the species;
- (e) the denomination of the Amateur Variety;
- (f) the words “Amateur Variety”;
- (g) the reference number of the lot given by the person responsible for affixing the labels;
- (h) the declared net or gross weight or declared number of seeds;
- (i) where weight is indicated and granulated pesticides, pelleting substances or other solid additives are used, the nature of the chemical treatment or additive and the approximate ratio between the weight of clusters of pure seeds and the total weight.

(This note is not part of the Regulations)

These Regulations re-enact, with amendments, the Vegetable Seeds Regulations 1985.

The Regulations, which come into force on 1st September 1993, continue to restrict the marketing in Great Britain of vegetable seeds to specified categories meeting prescribed standards (regulations 3(3), 5(1) and Schedule 4), to require such seeds to be sold in sealed packages labelled or marked with prescribed particulars (regulations 8 and 9 and Schedule 6) and to make tampering with labels prescribed by regulations an offence (regulation 9(13)). The Regulations also continue to make provision for the taking of samples for the verification of standards (regulation 7 and Schedule 5) and provide that certain particulars stated or implied on a package of seeds are deemed to constitute a statutory warranty by the seller for the protection of the purchaser for the purpose of section 17 of the Plant Varieties and Seeds Act 1964 (regulation 10(1)).

The consolidation incorporates a provision required under Council Directive [88/380/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1988/0380) (OJ No. L187, 16.7.88, p.31), and previously dealt with by a General Licence under Regulation 5(3)(d) of the 1985 Regulations. This relates to the marketing to other Member States of seed in respect of which an official certificate has not been issued (regulation 5(14) and Schedule 6 Part IC).

The Regulations implement Council Directive [70/458/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1970/0458) (OJ No. L225, 12.10.70, p.7) on the marketing of vegetable seed as amended.

[^f01000]: Footnote: (1) *(Extent: S)*[^key-75976b440fdf4bd0498d8229a801e44b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    For the categories Pre-basic Seed, Basic Seeds and Certified Seed of these kinds, the minimum submitted sample weight must be: Broad Bean 4000g, Climbing French Bean / Dwarf French Bean 3000g, Pea 2000g. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

[^f01001]: Footnote: (1) *(Extent: S)*[^key-e61725e4105b9127de400b2ef26f1856] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    For the categories Pre-basic Seed, Basic Seeds and Certified Seed of these kinds, the minimum submitted sample weight must be: Broad Bean 4000g, Climbing French Bean / Dwarf French Bean 3000g, all Pea groups 2000g. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

[^f01002]: Footnote: (2) *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]

    For the category Standards Seed of these kinds the minimum submitted sample weight must be 25g. *(Extent: S)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8acfaad93d54c6c80e180eca16a88c7c]
